Second:

```json
{"ref": "ssb.lump_sum"}
```

Second transcription's note: The tool does not compute the lump-sum election. Choosing it is refused, so whenever this line is computed the box is unchecked.

Ruling: representation; project retained. The election is for a social security benefit payment for an earlier year, so it can only apply to a filer with benefits; the project tests benefits first (the 'all' stops there) so a filer without benefits is never asked the lump-sum question, and for a filer with benefits the box is the answer itself.

Second transcription's note: Only the tax on taxable income is computed. Form 8814, Form 4972, section 962, education credit recapture, Form 8621 and Form 8978 amounts are refused through scope.other_line16. The worksheets treat Form 2555 filers differently (Foreign Earned Income Tax Worksheet); that case is refused through sa.excluded_income.

Ruling: scope and representation; project retained. As the earlier ruling: Form 8814, Form 4972 and the box 3 tax are refused through scope.other_line16. The second transcription now selects the QDCG worksheet by 'line 3a or QDCG line 3 more than zero'; QDCG line 3 is the smaller of Schedule D lines 15 and 16 when Schedule D is filed and box 2a otherwise, and the project's guard tests Schedule D line 17 (both gains) or, when no Schedule D is needed (no sales, g28.line7 and u1250.line18, which are Schedule D lines 18 and 19, both zero), capital gain distributions; sd.filed is defined by exactly those tests, so the selections agree. The Schedule D Tax Worksheet selection matches in substance.

```

Second transcription's note: A filer who chooses not to claim the EIC enters nothing on line 27a. This is read as zero.

Ruling: scope; project retained. The second adds the filer's own choice not to claim the EIC ('If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here', line 27c; i1040gi p. 40), which the project does not ask: the EIC is figured whenever the filer qualifies, and the box itself is tracked in TASKS.md T-027. Where the filer claims it, both give the same amount and the same STOPs that say to check line 27c.

### 27b — Clergy filing Schedule SE (see instructions)
Status: **AGREE**

### 27c — If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here
Status: **DISAGREE**

Second transcription's note: The line 27c instructions say to check the box if you do not want to claim the EIC, or if the line 27a instructions tell you to. Both cases are covered.

Ruling: scope; project retained. The second adds the filer's own choice not to claim the EIC ('If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here', line 27c; i1040gi p. 40), which the project does not ask: the EIC is figured whenever the filer qualifies, and the box itself is tracked in TASKS.md T-027. Where the filer claims it, both give the same amount and the same STOPs that say to check line 27c.

### Dependents (separated spouses) — Check if your filing status is MFS or HOH and you lived apart from your spouse for the las
Status: **AGREE**

### 28 — Additional child tax credit (ACTC) from Schedule 8812
Status: **AGREE**

### 29 — American opportunity credit from Form 8863, line 8
Status: **AGREE**

### 30 — Refundable adoption credit from Form 8839, line 13
Status: **AGREE**

### 31 — Amount from Schedule 3, line 15
Status: **AGREE**

### 32 — Add lines 27a, 28, 29, 30, and 31. These are your total other payments and refundable cred
Status: **AGREE**

### 33 — Add lines 25d, 26, and 32. These are your total payments
Status: **AGREE**

### 34 — If line 33 is more than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 33. This is the amount you ove
Status: **AGREE**

### 37 — Subtract line 33 from line 24. This is the amount you owe
Status: **AGREE**

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1z | scope.other_income | scope.other_income |
| 6c | ssb.lump_sum | ssb.lump_sum |
| 9 | scope.other_income | - |
| 11a | scope.other_income | - |
| 11b | scope.other_income | - |
| 15 | scope.other_income | - |
| 16 | sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16 | - |
| 18 | sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16 | - |
| 22 | sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16 | - |
| 24 | sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16 | - |
| 29 | scope.credits | - |
| 30 | scope.credits | - |
| 32 | scope.credits | - |
| 33 | scope.credits | - |
| 34 | sa.excluded_income, scope.credits, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16 | - |
| 37 | sa.excluded_income, scope.credits, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16 | - |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: f1040.line1a
    printed: "1a"
    label: "Total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.box1}
    note: "The instructions say to enter the total from Form(s) W-2, box 1, and to include the spouse's W-2s on a joint return. Each person's W-2 box 1 total (person.box1) is added up across the person entries."
  - id: f1040.line1z
    printed: "1z"
    label: "Add lines 1a through 1h"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1a}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "The tool does not compute lines 1b through 1h (household employee wages, unreported tips, Medicaid waiver payments, dependent care benefits, adoption benefits, Form 8919 wages, other earned income). They are left out and screened by scope.other_income, so line 1z equals line 1a."
  - id: f1040.line2a
    printed: "2a"
    label: "Tax-exempt interest"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.tax_exempt_interest, div.box12]}
    note: "The line 2a instructions also say to include exempt-interest dividends from Form 1099-DIV box 12. The supplied tax-exempt interest input does not include box 12, so it is added here."
  - id: f1040.line2b
    printed: "2b"
    label: "Taxable interest"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sb.line4}
  - id: f1040.line3b
    printed: "3b"
    label: "Ordinary dividends"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sb.line6}
  - id: f1040.line4a
    printed: "4a"
    label: "IRA distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.amount_a
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.line4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "IRA distributions, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.h_ira_rollover_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of IRA distributions with part or all rolled over"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.line4c
    printed: "4c"
    label: "Check if (box 1) Rollover"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1040.h_ira_rollover_count, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1040.line5a
    printed: "5a"
    label: "Pensions and annuities"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.amount_a
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
  - id: f1040.line5b
    printed: "5b"
    label: "Pensions and annuities, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
  - id: f1040.h_plan_rollover_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of pension and annuity distributions with a rollover"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.is_ira, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.has_rollover, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.line5c
    printed: "5c"
    label: "Check if (box 1) Rollover"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f1040.h_plan_rollover_count, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1040.line6a
    printed: "6a"
    label: "Social security benefits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.line1}
  - id: f1040.line6b
    printed: "6b"
    label: "Social security benefits, taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.line18}
  - id: f1040.line6c
    printed: "6c"
    label: "If you elect to use the lump-sum election method, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: ssb.lump_sum}
    refuses: [ssb.lump_sum]
    note: "The tool does not compute the lump-sum election. Choosing it is refused, so whenever this line is computed the box is unchecked."
  - id: f1040.line6d
    printed: "6d"
    label: "If you are married filing separately and lived apart from your spouse the entire year, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [ssb.lived_apart, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.line7a
    printed: "7a"
    label: "Capital gain or (loss). Attach Schedule D if required"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sd.to1040}
  - id: f1040.line8
    printed: "8"
    label: "Additional income from Schedule 1, line 10"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s1.line10}
  - id: f1040.line9
    printed: "9"
    label: "Add lines 1z, 2b, 3b, 4b, 5b, 6b, 7a, and 8. This is your total income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line1z, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, f1040.line4b, f1040.line5b, f1040.line6b, f1040.line7a, f1040.line8]}
  - id: f1040.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "Adjustments to income from Schedule 1, line 26"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s1.line26}
  - id: f1040.line11a
    printed: "11a"
    label: "Subtract line 10 from line 9. This is your adjusted gross income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: f1040.line9, take: f1040.line10}
  - id: f1040.line11b
    printed: "11b"
    label: "Amount from line 11a (adjusted gross income)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11a}
  - id: f1040.line12e
    printed: "12e"
    label: "Standard deduction or itemized deductions (from Schedule A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: sa.deduction}
  - id: f1040.line13a
    printed: "13a"
    label: "Qualified business income deduction from Form 8995 or Form 8995-A"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: qbi.deduction}
  - id: f1040.line13b
    printed: "13b"
    label: "Additional deductions from Schedule 1-A, line 38"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s1a.line38}
  - id: f1040.line14
    printed: "14"
    label: "Add lines 12e, 13a, and 13b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line12e, f1040.line13a, f1040.line13b]}
  - id: f1040.line15
    printed: "15"
    label: "Subtract line 14 from line 11b. If zero or less, enter -0-. This is your taxable income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line11b, take: f1040.line14}
  - id: f1040.h_use_sdtw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Complete the Schedule D Tax Worksheet: Schedule D line 18 or 19 more than zero and lines 15 and 16 gains, or filing Form 4952 with an amount on line 4g"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - op: test
              any:
                - {op: test, gt: [sd.line18, {const: 0}]}
                - {op: test, gt: [sd.line19, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [sd.line17, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [sd.filing4952, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [sd.investment_election, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1040.h_no_worksheet
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Exception: use neither the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet nor the Schedule D Tax Worksheet"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, le: [f1040.line15, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, le: [qdcg.line3, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.line3a, {const: 0}]}
    note: "The exception text speaks of Schedule D lines 15 and 16 being zero or less, with no qualified dividends. QDCG Worksheet line 3 is used as the test amount: when Schedule D is filed it is the smaller of lines 15 and 16 (zero if either is a loss), and when it is not filed it is line 7a. This also covers a filer whose only gains are capital gain distributions reported without Schedule D. The line 16 instructions that choose the method are not in the text provided."
  - id: f1040.h_tax_table
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Tax on line 15 using the Tax Table or Tax Computation Worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: {def: tax_on_amount, bind: {amount: f1040.line15, status: f1040.status}, line: tax}}
  - id: f1040.line16
    printed: "16"
    label: "Tax (see instructions). Check if any from Form(s): 1 8814 2 4972 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_no_worksheet, {const: true}]}
          then: f1040.h_tax_table
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_no_worksheet, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_sdtw, {const: true}]}
          then: sdtw.line47
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_no_worksheet, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_sdtw, {const: false}]}
          then: qdcg.line25
    refuses: [scope.other_line16, sa.excluded_income]
    note: "Only the tax on taxable income is computed. Form 8814, Form 4972, section 962, education credit recapture, Form 8621 and Form 8978 amounts are refused through scope.other_line16. The worksheets treat Form 2555 filers differently (Foreign Earned Income Tax Worksheet); that case is refused through sa.excluded_income."
  - id: f1040.line17
    printed: "17"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 2, line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s2.line3}
  - id: f1040.line18
    printed: "18"
    label: "Add lines 16 and 17"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line16, f1040.line17]}
  - id: f1040.line19
    printed: "19"
    label: "Child tax credit or credit for other dependents from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.line14}
  - id: f1040.line20
    printed: "20"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s3.line8}
  - id: f1040.line21
    printed: "21"
    label: "Add lines 19 and 20"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line19, f1040.line20]}
  - id: f1040.line22
    printed: "22"
    label: "Subtract line 21 from line 18. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line18, take: f1040.line21}
  - id: f1040.line23
    printed: "23"
    label: "Other taxes, including self-employment tax, from Schedule 2, line 21"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s2.line21}
  - id: f1040.line24
    printed: "24"
    label: "Add lines 22 and 23. This is your total tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line22, f1040.line23]}
  - id: f1040.line25a
    printed: "25a"
    label: "Federal income tax withheld from Form(s) W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.box2}
  - id: f1040.line25d
    printed: "25d"
    label: "Add lines 25a through 25c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line25a, f1040.line25b, f1040.line25c]}
  - id: f1040.h_eic_decline
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Do you not want to claim the earned income credit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: input, source: "Interview question: Form 1040 line 27c, check if you do not want to claim the EIC"}
  - id: f1040.line27c
    printed: "27c"
    label: "If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_eic_decline, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: true}]}
    note: "The line 27c instructions say to check the box if you do not want to claim the EIC, or if the line 27a instructions tell you to. Both cases are covered."
  - id: f1040.line27a
    printed: "27a"
    label: "Earned income credit (EIC)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_eic_decline, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_eic_decline, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.credit
    note: "A filer who chooses not to claim the EIC enters nothing on line 27a. This is read as zero."
  - id: f1040.line27b
    printed: "27b"
    label: "Clergy filing Schedule SE (see instructions)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.clergy}
  - id: "f1040.lineDependents (separated spouses)"
    printed: "Dependents (separated spouses)"
    label: "Check if your filing status is MFS or HOH and you lived apart from your spouse for the last 6 months of 2025, or you are legally separated according to your state law under a written separation agreement or a decree of separate maintenance and you did not live in the same household as your spouse at the end of 2025."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.separated_box}
  - id: f1040.line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Additional child tax credit (ACTC) from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s8812.line27}
  - id: f1040.line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "American opportunity credit from Form 8863, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.credits]
    note: "The tool does not compute Form 8863. Claiming the credit is refused, so whenever this line is computed it has no amount."
  - id: f1040.line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "Refundable adoption credit from Form 8839, line 13"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sales.zero}
    refuses: [scope.credits]
    note: "The tool does not compute Form 8839. Claiming the credit is refused, so whenever this line is computed it has no amount."
  - id: f1040.line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 15"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: s3.line15}
  - id: f1040.line32
    printed: "32"
    label: "Add lines 27a, 28, 29, 30, and 31. These are your total other payments and refundable credits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line27a, f1040.line28, f1040.line29, f1040.line30, f1040.line31]}
  - id: f1040.line33
    printed: "33"
    label: "Add lines 25d, 26, and 32. These are your total payments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line25d, f1040.line26, f1040.line32]}
  - id: f1040.line34
    printed: "34"
    label: "If line 33 is more than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 33. This is the amount you overpaid"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line33, take: f1040.line24}
    note: "When line 33 is not more than line 24, the line is left blank. This is read as zero."
  - id: f1040.line37
    printed: "37"
    label: "Subtract line 33 from line 24. This is the amount you owe"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line24, take: f1040.line33}
    note: "The form gives no rule for the case where payments are more than the tax (an overpayment, shown on line 34). The amount owed is read as zero then, not as a negative number."
```
